## PREMISES ALREADY SETTLED, RE-INLINED (each is a QUESTION if you can check it)

1. **43 of the 0x40 byte offsets reach `default:`** -- 18 named unimplemented registers, 12 counter-word interior
   bytes, 13 unnamed (derived from mvme_pcctworeg.h against the 21 case labels; two seats re-derived it).
2. **Reads are served BEFORE the switch** by the memcpy (with the #119 end-span guard: a read crossing 0x40 is
   zero-filled, not served -- irrelevant to byte offsets, and premise 2 of the pass-1 brief was over-general there).
3. **The witness** (`witness_20260905/`): on the committed build a GUEST `st.b` or `ld.bu` to IEICR ends the host
   (exit 1) with the arm's own `pcc2: unimplemented` line first; IEBERR, the in-word byte 0x05 and the alias 0x6a
   likewise; the debugger's `put b` does NOT reach the arm (a control -- `put b` never reaches a DM_DEFAULT device).
   7/13 pre-fix, 13/13 post-fix (both inlined).  The IEBERR/IEERR row measures IEBERR only (the exiting build dies at
   the first); IEERR's kill follows from source (codex).  **The witness is DISPOSITION-BLIND** (Opus built both
   candidates: 13/13 under decline and under backed-accept) -- the DETECTOR's `ieicr rdawr`/`ieerr rdawr` rows (store
   0x5a then load in ONE session, want 0x0) are what pin the disposition; `sccerr rdawr` (a CASED write-to-clear
   sibling, 0x0 under either) is the control that makes the delta attributable.
4. **The guest driver never attaches on this model**: `machine_mvme88k.c:106` installs `unreadable` at 0xfff46000
   (the ie0 address), `if_ie.c:407-409` fails its match on `badpaddr()` there, and `ie_setupram()` returns early
   at `:561-564` before the PCC2 store at `:609`.  `ie0` IS configured in `conf/M187` and `conf/NFS` (the pass-1
   brief's conf grep looked in the wrong files).  So the HIGH ranking rests on the witness and severity.
5. **The alias is DOCUMENTED**: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists `$FFF42040-$FFF42FFF  PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated`
   (`_scratchpad/vmesbcp2.txt` line 4629), so `relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE` models the part.  The fork's
   `PCC2_SIZE` (0x40) and the guest header's (0x1000) name different things.
6. **The design, converged**: READ side keeps the byte the memcpy served and says so (`served 0x%02x` -- it is the
   array's default, NOT a computed value: not member 4 of the option set); WRITE side member 3 -- DECLINE + latched
   `fatal()` `(once)`, per OFFSET (one `uint64_t` bit per byte offset; `relative_addr` is folded to 0..0x3f above
   the switch so the shift is defined).  NOT member 6 (backed accept): the cased siblings contradict a blanket store
   (SCCERR is write-to-clear -- the manual's SCLR bits read as zero; the ICRs mask + reassert; PSCALECNT/SPEED/the
   IACKs are read-only), the addition licence is unmet, and a store from the one arm 43 offsets reach adds a
   bounded-write surface.  The codex seat's alternative -- ONE latch for the whole site, writes only, reads keep
   the old `debug()` -- is RECORDED, not adopted (per-offset announces are what a driver scanning registers needs;
   both are bounded).
7. **The IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest**; modelling them (on the SCCRXIACK shape) would open
   two new entries into the C040 path, so `pcc2c040kill` goes first (filed as a cross-round obligation).  The
   in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters -- declined here as a width matter, disclosed, not
   claimed as unimplemented registers.
8. **`return 0` denotes a FAILED TRANSACTION**, not inherently unmapped hardware (an IACK cycle with no interrupt
   pending times out on the real part) -- a correction to the option set's member-5 wording (codex).
9. **The roster**: all nine seats; agy DELIBERATELY NOT FIRED (measurement reserve).

## THE HANDLER AS IT NOW STANDS (`DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2)`, VERBATIM -- every exit path)

```c
DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2)
{
	uint64_t idata = 0;
	struct pcc2_data *d = (struct pcc2_data *) extra;

	/*  0xfff42000..0xfff42fff, but only 0x40 unique registers:  */
	relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE;

	if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
		idata = memory_readmax64(cpu, data, len);

	if (writeflag == MEM_READ) {
		if (relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
			memcpy(data, d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, len);
		else {
			static int warned = 0;	/* #119: loud-once (course-correction) */
			if (!warned) {
				fatal("[ pcc2: read 0x%x+%i beyond reg space 0x%x; "
				    "zero-filled ]\n", (int)relative_addr, (int)len,
				    (int)PCC2_SIZE);
				warned = 1;
			}
			memset(data, 0, len);	/* OB-11b: don't leak uninit host memory */
		}
	}

	switch (relative_addr) {

	case PCCTWO_CHIPID:
	case PCCTWO_CHIPREV:
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			fatal("[ IGNORING write to read-only PCCTWO_CHIPID/"
			    "CHIPREV register ]\n");
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_GENCTL:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_GENCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_GENCTL", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata;
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_VECBASE:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN, "PCCTWO_VECBASE", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xf0;
			if (idata & ~0xf0)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_VECBASE"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_T1CMP:
	case PCCTWO_T2CMP:
	case PCCTWO_T1COUNT:
	case PCCTWO_T2COUNT:
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE &&
		    relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
			memcpy(d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, data, len);
		break;

	case PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_PSCALEADJ_LEN, "PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			fatal("[ pcc2: write to PSCALEADJ ignored (value "
			    "0x%02x) ]\n", (int)idata);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_T2CTL:
	case PCCTWO_T1CTL:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_TXCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_TxCTL", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			/*  PCC2_TCTL_CEN and PCC2_TCTL_COC:  */
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= 0xfc;
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] |= (idata & 3);

			if (idata & PCC2_TCTL_COVF)
				d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &=
				    ~(PCC2_TCTL_OVF | PCC2_TCTL_COVF);

			if (idata & ~7)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_TxCTL"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_T2ICR:
	case PCCTWO_T1ICR:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_TXICR_LEN, "PCCTWO_TxICR", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= ~0x17;
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] |= (idata & 0x17);

			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T1ICR &&
			    !(idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_IEN))
				d->pending_timer1_interrupts = 0;
			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T2ICR &&
			    !(idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_IEN))
				d->pending_timer2_interrupts = 0;

			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T1ICR && (idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_ICLR)
			    && d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] & PCC2_TTIRQ_INT
			    && d->pending_timer1_interrupts > 0)
				d->pending_timer1_interrupts --;

			if (relative_addr == PCCTWO_T2ICR && (idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_ICLR)
			    && d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] & PCC2_TTIRQ_INT
			    && d->pending_timer2_interrupts > 0)
				d->pending_timer2_interrupts --;

			if (idata & PCC2_TTIRQ_ICLR)
				d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= ~PCC2_TTIRQ_INT;

			if (idata & 0xe0)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_TxICR"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);

			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCERR:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCCERR_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCCERR", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = 0;	/*  clear  */
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCICR:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCCICR_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCCICR", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0x1f;
			if (idata & ~0x1f)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCCICR"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCTX:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCCTX_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCCTX", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0x1f;
			if (idata & ~0x1f)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCCTX"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCRX:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCCRX_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCCRX", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xdf;
			if (idata & ~0xdf)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCCRX"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCCRXIACK:
		/*  TODO. Hm. Is this enough?  */
		d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_SCCRX] &= ~PCC2_IRQ_INT;
		reassert_interrupts(d);
		break;

	case PCCTWO_SCSIICR:
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_SCSIICR_LEN, "PCCTWO_SCSIICR", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xdf;
			if (idata & ~0xdf)
				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_SCSIICR"
				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_IPL:
		/*  pcc2widthkills: was a #119 `static int w' -- process-global, and it
		    said "ignored" about a READ the memcpy above had already served.
		    Now per-instance, write-only, like the ten blocks above -- that is
		    the WIDTH latch; the body's own #119 `static int w' below stays
		    process-global (pcc2pscaleflood's family; not this round's).  */
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_IPL_LEN, "PCCTWO_IPL", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			static int w = 0;	/* #119: was exit(1) (IPL is read-only) */
			if (!w) { fatal("[ pcc2: write to read-only PCCTWO_IPL; "
			    "ignored ]\n"); w = 1; }
		} else {
			if (d->pcctwo_reg[PCCTWO_IPL] == 0) {
				static int w = 0;	/* #119: was exit(1) */
				if (!w) { fatal("[ pcc2: IPL == 0 on read ]\n");
				    w = 1; }
			}
		}
		break;

	case PCCTWO_MASK:
		/*  pcc2widthkills: was a #119 `static int w' -- process-global, and it
		    said "ignored" about a READ the memcpy above had already served.
		    Now per-instance, write-only, like the ten blocks above -- that is
		    the WIDTH latch.  (pcc2widthkills pass 7: this comment once claimed a
		    #119 static of MASK's own "below" -- there is none; the body stores
		    the byte and reasserts.  The static belongs to IPL's arm above.)  */
		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_MASK_LEN, "PCCTWO_MASK", len);
			break;
		}
		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata;
			reassert_interrupts(d);
		}
		break;

	default:
		/*  #501 (pcc2defaultkill): this arm ended the host with exit(1) on ANY
		    access to an offset with no case -- 43 of the 0x40 byte offsets:
		    eighteen documented registers the model does not implement (IEICR,
		    IEERR, IEBERR, GPIO, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, the *IACKs),
		    the bytes inside the four counter/compare words, and the unnamed
		    ones -- READS included, though the memcpy above the switch had
		    already served them.  A guest ld.bu or st.b at any of them was a host
		    kill (rung-3 witness).  Now: a READ keeps the served byte (the array's
		    default, said so -- not a computed value); a WRITE is DECLINED.  Not a
		    backed store: the cased siblings contradict one (SCCERR clears on
		    write, the ICRs mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are
		    read-only), no guest reaches these offsets on this model (if_ie never
		    attaches: unreadable sits at its 82596 address), and a store from the
		    one arm 43 offsets reach would add a bounded-write surface.  One
		    latched fatal() per offset per instance, the house `(once)' shape --
		    unlike PSCALEADJ's UNLATCHED print four arms up (pcc2pscaleflood).  The
		    IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest: modelling them (on
		    the SCCRXIACK shape) would open two new entries into the C040 path,
		    so pcc2c040kill goes first.  The %= fold above IS the real part's:
		    Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists $FFF42040-$FFF42FFF as repeated
		    PCCchip2 registers.  */
		if (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {
			d->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;
			if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "
				    "0x%x: 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\n",
				    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);
			else
				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "
				    "0x%x -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\n",
				    (int) relative_addr,
				    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);
		}
		break;
	}

	return 1;
}
```

## THE DETECTOR (`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, VERBATIM, contract included)

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""pcc2defaultkill DETECTOR (#501) -- the `default:' arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) in dev_pcc2.c.

Before #501 that arm ended the emulator process (`exit(1)') on ANY access to an offset with no `case'
-- READS included, although the memcpy above the switch had already served them.  Forty-three of the
0x40 byte offsets reach it (derived from mvme_pcctworeg.h against the case list): eighteen NAMED
registers the model does not implement (PSCALECNT, GPIO_ICR/PCR, SCCMOIACK, SCCTXIACK, IEERR, IEICR,
IEBERR, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, PRTDATA), the bytes INSIDE the four 32-bit counter/compare
words, and the unnamed offsets.  A single guest `st.b' or `ld.bu' at any of them was a host kill
(rung-3 witness, 7/13 on the committed build: regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/).

THE FIX (pass-1 panel of 2026-09-05, nine seats, converged): READ side -- keep the byte the memcpy
already served (it is the register array's default, NOT a computed value: the announce says so);
WRITE side -- DECLINE + a per-offset latched `fatal()' ("(once)", the house idiom), NOT a backed
accept: the file's own cased siblings contradict a blanket store (SCCERR is write-to-clear, the ICRs
mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are read-only), the addition licence is unmet (no guest
reaches these offsets on this model -- if_ie never attaches), and a store into pcctwo_reg[] from the
one arm reached by 43 offsets adds a bounded-write surface the decline does not have.

CONTRACT -- what this detector is built to catch, by row:
  (b) the verbatim `exit(1)' reinstated             -> every survival row (named wr/rd, inword, unnamed, alias)
  (d) the announce unlatched                        -> `latch sameoff' (2 lines, not 1)
  (d) one whole-arm latch bit instead of per-offset -> `latch twooff' (1 line, not 2), `named wr msg' (1, not 18)
  (d) a writeflag guard that re-kills READS         -> `named rd' (host exit)
  (d) the direction test inverted in the text       -> `dir wr text' / `dir rd text'
  member 6 (BACKED ACCEPT) substituted for member 3 -> `ieicr rdawr' / `ieerr rdawr' read back the stored
      byte (0x5a) instead of 0x0 -- the DISPOSITION-pinning rows; `sccerr rdawr' (a CASED, write-to-clear
      sibling) is the control that reads 0x0 under either and makes the delta attributable
  the `relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE' fold changed      -> `alias msg' (the announce names offset 0x2a, not 0x6a)
  the fix perturbing a cased arm                    -> `genctl rt', `t1cmp word rt'
DISCLOSED, not caught: the in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters, declined here as a
width/alignment matter (pcc2widecompose's business), not as unimplemented registers; the fold of
0x40..0xfff onto 0x00..0x3f IS the real part's: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists $FFF42040-$FFF42FFF as
'PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated' (_scratchpad/vmesbcp2.txt:4629, fetched at the codex seat's citation -- two
seats had rightly called it unestablished before the manual was on disk), so `alias 0x6a' pins a documented
behaviour; the fork's PCC2_SIZE (0x40) and the guest header's (0x1000) name different things;
the IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest -- modelling them (on the SCCRXIACK shape) would
open two new entries into the C040 path, so pcc2c040kill goes first; `named wr'/`named rd' touch all
eighteen offsets in ONE session each (valid because the fix has no per-offset branch; `named wr msg'
localises a failure) -- if a later round branches per offset this compression is invalid.
The debugger's `put b' never reaches a DM_DEFAULT arm (measured): `put b ctl' is a control, not a witness.

Usage: python3 pcc2_default_probe.py <gxemul> <label>      Sentinel: PCC2D_PROBE_DONE
The pty session helper is the width probe's own (extracted at run time), never hand-rolled: a first
draft of the witness hand-rolled it and read every host kill as "survived".
"""
import io, os, pty, re, select, sys, time
BIN = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "build/gxemul"
LABEL = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "local"
DUMMY = "/tmp/pcc2_dummy.bin"
if not os.path.exists(DUMMY):
    open(DUMMY, "wb").write(b"\0" * 4096)
HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
_w = io.open(os.path.join(HERE, "pcc2_width_probe.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
exec(_w[_w.index("def session("):_w.index("\ndef printed")])     # session(cmds) -> (transcript, alive, how)

CODE, SCRATCH, PCC2, SENTINEL = 0x00010000, 0x00020000, 0xfff42000, 0xdeadbeef
CHIPID, GENCTL, T1CMP, SCCERR = 0x00, 0x02, 0x04, 0x1c
IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCCTXIACK, PRTICR = 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x25, 0x30
NAMED18 = [0x14, 0x18, 0x19, 0x23, 0x25, 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x2c, 0x30, 0x31, 0x32, 0x33, 0x34, 0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x3a]
INWORD = [0x05, 0x09, 0x0d, 0x11]
UNNAMED = [0x20, 0x24, 0x29, 0x35, 0x3d]
MSG = "pcc2: unimplemented"
def enc(op26, rd, rs1, imm): return (op26 << 26) | (rd << 21) | (rs1 << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)
def LD_BU(rd, off): return enc(0x03, rd, 2, off)
def LD_W(rd, off): return enc(0x05, rd, 2, off)
def ST_B(rs, off): return enc(0x0b, rs, 2, off)
def ST_W(rs, off): return enc(0x09, rs, 2, off)
I_ST_SCR = enc(0x09, 5, 3, 0); I_LD_SCR = enc(0x05, 4, 3, 0); I_BR_SELF = 0xc0000000
def program(insns, at=CODE):
    words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
    return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * k, w) for k, w in enumerate(words)] + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]
def setup(r5="0x5a5a5a5a"):
    return ["r2=0x%x" % PCC2, "r3=0x%x" % SCRATCH, "r5=%s" % r5, "r4=0x%x" % SENTINEL]
def printed(t, reg="r4"):
    m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\r?\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
    return "0x%x" % int(m.group(1), 16) if m else "absent"
def fate(alive, how): return "survived" if alive else "host " + how
def val(t, alive, how): return printed(t) if alive else fate(alive, how)
rows, bad = [], []
def row(name, got, want, why):
    ok = str(got) == str(want)
    rows.append(name); (None if ok else bad.append(name))
    #  STATUS LAST: gate 14 counts a row with `^<name>  .*ok$' / ` ok$' / ` FAIL$' -- a first draft printed the
    #  status before the note and the gate counted ZERO of 23 rows while the probe said DONE (caught by the gate, L1).
    print("%-16s %-14s %-14s %-60s %s" % (name, got, want, why[:60], "ok" if ok else "FAIL"), flush=True)
print("pion(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEERR), LD_BU(4, IEERR)]) + ["print r4"])
row("ieerr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "the same at IEERR (0x28), the write-to-clear class whose cased sibling SCCERR clears on write")
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, SCCERR), LD_BU(4, SCCERR)]) + ["print r4"])
row("sccerr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "CONTROL: the CASED write-to-clear sibling reads 0x0 under either disposition -- makes the two rows above attributable")
# -- the latch: per offset, per instance
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEICR)]))
row("latch sameoff", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "two writes to one offset announce ONCE: an unlatched arm prints 2")
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEBERR)]))
row("latch twooff", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 2, "two offsets announce TWICE: a single whole-arm latch bit prints 1")
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), LD_BU(4, IEICR)]))
row("latch rw one", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "a write then a read at ONE offset announce once: the latch is per offset, not per direction")
# -- the fold and the text
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, 0x6a)]))
row("alias 0x6a", fate(alive, how), "survived", "0x6a folds onto 0x2a (`relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE'): the MODEL's fold, not proven of the real part")
row("alias msg", t.count("offset 0x2a") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and the announce names the FOLDED offset 0x2a: a changed fold names another")
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, PRTICR)]))
row("dir wr text", t.count("unimplemented write to") if alive else "n/a", 1, "the announce says WRITE for a store (an inverted writeflag test in the text says read)")
t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, PRTICR)]))
row("dir rd text", t.count("unimplemented read from") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and READ for a load")
# -- the cased arms are untouched
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x82") + program([ST_B(5, GENCTL), LD_BU(4, GENCTL)]) + ["print r4"])
row("genctl rt", val(t, alive, how), "0x82", "an IMPLEMENTED byte register still round-trips (no C040 bit in 0x82)")
t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12345678") + program([ST_W(5, T1CMP), LD_W(4, T1CMP)]) + ["print r4"])
row("t1cmp word rt", val(t, alive, how), "0x12345678", "the cased 32-bit counter arm still round-trips a word: the fix swallowed no cased arm")
t, alive, how = session(["put b 0xfff4202a, 0x00"])
row("put b ctl", fate(alive, how), "survived", "CONTROL: the debugger's put b never reaches a DM_DEFAULT arm (measured before the fix too)")
row("put b ctl msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 0, "...so it prints nothing -- not a witness, a control")
print("PCC2D_RESULT=%d/%d" % (len(rows) - len(bad), len(rows)))
if bad:
    print("PCC2D_RED=%s" % ",".join(bad))
print("PCC2D_PROBE_DONE")

```

## THE CHANGELOG BLOCK (as it stands)

```
# Round #501 pcc2defaultkill -- the `default:` arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) ended the host on any access to an uncased offset, reads included; now reads keep the served byte and writes are declined, one latched announce per offset

**2026-09-05.  HIGH, ranked first by the 2026-09-04 fix-review adjudication.  Product change: `src/devices/dev_pcc2.c`
-- the `exit(1)` replaced by a per-offset latched `fatal()` (`uint64_t warned_unimpl`, one bit per byte offset) that
says DECLINED for a write and SERVED 0x%02x for a read, then `break` to the handler's single `return 1`.  Detector:
`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, 23 rows, run by gate 14.  Witness: rung 3, measured before the edit.**

## The defect and its witness

Forty-three of the 0x40 byte offsets have no `case` (derived from `mvme_pcctworeg.h` against the 21 labels; two seats
re-derived it): eighteen NAMED registers the model does not implement (PSCALECNT, GPIO_ICR/PCR, SCCMOIACK, SCCTXIACK,
IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, PRTDATA), the twelve bytes INSIDE the four 32-bit
counter/compare words, and thirteen unnamed offsets.  Any access to one of them -- a READ included, although the
memcpy above the switch had already served it -- reached `default:` and `exit(1)`.

**The witness (`regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/`), measured on the committed build BEFORE the
edit: 7/13.**  A guest `st.b` to IEICR (0x2a) ends the host with exit status 1, the arm's own `pcc2: unimplemented`
line printed first (the arm was reached); a guest `ld.bu` there likewise -- the READ side kills too; IEBERR and the
in-word byte 0x05 and the alias 0x6a kill the same way.  Liveness (the probe's scratch round-trip) and
device-signature (CHIPID reads PCC2_ID 0x20) controls pass.  **The debugger's `put b` does NOT reach the arm** -- it
survives and prints nothing -- which the filing had called the cheapest witness (measured false; corrected).  The
witness script is the width probe's own `session()` helper: a first draft hand-rolled the pty wait, accepted a
STALE prompt, and read every kill as "survived" -- the carrier's rule about hand-rolled pty plumbing, re-learned.
The combined IEBERR/IEERR row measures IEBERR only (the exiting build dies at the first); IEERR's kill follows from
source (codex).  **13/13 on the fixed build.**

## The design (pass 1: nine seats, `_scratchpad/panel_20260905_021751`, converged)

READ: keep the byte the memcpy served and SAY it is served -- it is the register array's default, not a computed
value, so this is not the option set's member 4 and a bounded announce about it is not a wrong record.  WRITE:
member 3, DECLINE + latched announce -- NOT member 6 (backed accept), on three warrants the seats measured or read:
the file's own cased siblings contradict a blanket store (SCCERR is write-to-clear -- the board manual's SCLR bits
read as zero; the ICRs mask and reassert; PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are read-only); the addition licence is unmet
(no guest reaches these offsets on this model); a store from the one arm 43 offsets reach adds a bounded-write
surface into `pcctwo_reg[]` at 0x3d.  The Opus seat BUILT both candidates in a scratch clone and measured the
13-row witness at 13/13 under BOTH -- disposition-blind -- so the detector carries read-after-write rows in ONE
session (`ieicr rdawr`, `ieerr rdawr`: store 0x5a then load, want 0x0 -- a backed accept reads 0x5a) with the CASED
write-to-clear sibling `sccerr rdawr` as the control that reads 0x0 under either.

**Two premises of the pass-1 brief were wrong and are corrected here.**  The in-tree guest driver (`if_ie.c`) writes
three of these offsets, but NEVER RUNS on this model: `machine_mvme88k.c:106` installs `unreadable` at the 82596
address, `if_ie.c:407-409` fails its match on `badpaddr()` there, and `ie_setupram()` returns early at `:561-564`
before the PCC2 store at `:609` (Fable, Opus, grok, codex independently; verified).  `ie0` IS configured in
`conf/M187` and `conf/NFS` -- the brief's conf grep looked in the wrong files.  So the HIGH ranking rests on the
witness and severity, not on the driver.  And the alias the model implements (`relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE`) is the
REAL part's: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists `$FFF42040-$FFF42FFF  PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated` -- the codex seat
cited it, the manual was fetched into `_scratchpad/` and the line read (`vmesbcp2.txt:4629`).  Two seats had rightly
called the fold unestablished before the manual was on disk.

Recorded alternatives: the codex seat's ONE latch for the whole site, writes only (not adopted: a driver scanning
registers should see each distinct one announced; both forms are bounded).  Corrections to the option set from this
round: `return 0` denotes a FAILED TRANSACTION, not inherently unmapped hardware (an IACK cycle with no interrupt
pending times out on the real part).  Disclosed: the in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters, declined
here as a width/alignment matter (`pcc2widecompose`), not claimed as unimplemented registers; the IACK offsets
0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest -- modelling them on the SCCRXIACK shape would open two new entries into the
C040 path, so `pcc2c040kill` goes first (filed as a cross-round obligation); per-instance latch scope is not
provable by two fresh sessions (each is a new process) -- a two-instance fixture is filed.

## The detector (`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, 23 rows; gate 14 pins them)

Controls: `live`, `devsig chipid`, `sccerr rdawr` (cased sibling), `genctl rt`, `t1cmp word rt` (the cased arms are
untouched), `put b ctl` + `put b ctl msg` (the debugger path never reaches the arm).  Survival: `named wr`, `named rd`
(all eighteen named offsets in ONE session per direction -- valid because the fix has no per-offset branch;
`named wr msg` localises), `inword wr`, `unnamed wr`, `alias 0x6a`.  Disposition: `ieicr rdawr`, `ieerr rdawr` (0x0,
not the stored byte), `named rd srvd` (the read announce says SERVED, never ignored).  Latch: `named wr msg` 18,
`named rd msg` 18, `latch sameoff` 1, `latch twooff` 2, `latch rw one` 1.  Text: `alias msg` (the announce names the
FOLDED offset), `dir wr text`, `dir rd text`.  The pty helper is the width probe's, extracted at run time -- the
frozen instrument is therefore a PAIR (both files copied beside the table).

## The kill table (`regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/killtable_20260905/driver.log`; predictions in `PRETABLE_501.out`, mtime 04:12:20; table launched 04:12:43; frozen instrument pair md5 c76e053a / a080e2bc == live)

| run | predicted | measured | red rows |
|---|---|---|---|
| BASE (fresh clone of build/, rebuilt) | 23/23 | **23/23** | -- |
| `M_EXIT` -- the verbatim `exit(1)` body reinstated | 8/23 | **7/23** | named wr, named wr msg, named rd, named rd msg, named rd srvd, inword wr, unnamed wr, ieicr rdawr, ieerr rdawr, latch sameoff, latch twooff, latch rw one, alias 0x6a, alias msg, dir wr text, dir rd text -- sixteen (the prediction listed fifteen and miscounted its own green set) |
| `M_UNLATCH` -- `if (1)` for the latch test | 21/23 | **21/23** | latch sameoff 2, latch rw one 2 -- exact |
| `M_ONELATCH` -- one whole-arm bit (`1ULL`) | 19/23 | **19/23** | named wr msg 1, named rd msg 1, named rd srvd 1, latch twooff 1 -- exact |
| `M_BACKED` -- a store into `pcctwo_reg[]` added (member 6) | 21/23 | **21/23** | ieicr rdawr 0x5a, ieerr rdawr 0x5a -- exact; `sccerr rdawr` (the cased write-to-clear control) stayed 0x0 |
| `M_RDKILL` -- `if (writeflag == MEM_READ) exit(1);` | 18/23 | **16/23** | named rd, named rd msg, named rd srvd, dir rd text, latch rw one (the read in that session exits) -- as predicted -- plus ieicr rdawr and ieerr rdawr, whose store-then-LOAD sessions exit at the load |
| `M_DIRSWAP` -- the announce's direction test inverted | 20/23 | **20/23** | dir wr text 0, dir rd text 0, named rd srvd 0 -- exact |

Six of six killed; three predictions exact, two missed in the SAFE direction (more reds), each derived: M_EXIT's
prediction listed fifteen red rows against a green set of seven and so summed to 22 of 23 -- an arithmetic slip in the
pre-table, not a channel surprise; M_RDKILL's read-after-write rows contain a LOAD, so they die with the reads.  Every
mutant string was gcc-syntax-checked before the table (`mut_syntax2.py`, anchors from the driver's own `mut` lines) and
proven by a diff count against SEC; the clone restored from SEC, rebuilt, cmp-clean, sentinel cleared.  The
disposition-pinning rows did their one job: `M_BACKED` reads back the stored byte at both uncased offsets while the
cased sibling reads 0x0 -- the difference between member 6 and member 3, measured, with its control.

**Two runs, one prediction file.**  Run 1 (04:12-04:25, frozen default probe `c76e053a`) is the table above; its row
PRINT format put the status before the note, and gate 14 -- which counts rows by `^<name>  .*ok$` -- then counted
ZERO of 23 rows while the probe said DONE (`killtable_20260905/gate14_501_FAIL_rowformat.log`, 25 of 844 checks
red, the 251 width rows all green).  L1, fixed in-round: status last, as the width probe prints; the gate's own
patterns then count 23.  Run 2 (05:05-05:18, frozen `a96b8bcf`) re-measured the same six mutants on the fixed-format
instrument -- BASE 23, M_EXIT 7, M_UNLATCH 21, M_ONELATCH 19, M_BACKED 21, M_RDKILL 16, M_DIRSWAP 20 -- identical
to run 1 row for row; run 1 is kept as `run1_oldrowformat/`.  *A probe whose summary line is right and whose rows
the gate cannot count is a detector that would have shipped green and counted nothing; the gate is the check
that saw it, which is what a gate is for.*

## Gate 14 (`killtable_20260905/gate14_501.log`; the live probes -- default md5 a96b8bcf, width a080e2bc, both equal to the frozen run-2 pair; run alone 05:19-05:55)

`gate 14: ARM/Thumb flags: PASS (844 checks)`, `pcc2 default rows emitted 23`, `pcc2 default rows green 23`, `pcc2 width rows emitted 251`,
`pcc2 width rows green 251`, GATE_RC=0.  The previous run of this gate (04:26-05:00, `gate14_501_FAIL_rowformat.log`) had
counted ZERO default rows against a probe that said DONE -- the row-format defect described above -- and is kept.

## Records

semantics on the SCCRXIACK shape, AFTER `pcc2c040kill`), `pcc2latchinst` (a two-instance fixture for per-instance
latch scope, a harness debt shared with the width latch).  `pcc2c040kill` gains the fact the design panel found: the
SCCRXIACK arm runs on READS (no writeflag test) and the console driver reads that offset, so the ENTRY into the C040
path is routine guest traffic -- only the arming (C040 set in GENCTL) is rare.  Ledger: witness
`probe:regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/defaultkill_witness.py`, detector
`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, every seat of both passes by name.  Carrier: the MVME187 manual added to the primary
sources; the member-5 wording.
```

## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)

**Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.

If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
far more to this panel than a timeout.

 222: +		    eighteen documented registers the model does not implement (IEICR,
 223: +		    IEERR, IEBERR, GPIO, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, the *IACKs),
 224: +		    the bytes inside the four counter/compare words, and the unnamed

 260: ## THE HANDLER AS IT NOW STANDS (`DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2)`, VERBATIM -- every exit path)
 261: 
 262: ```c
 263: DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2)
 264: {
 265: 	uint64_t idata = 0;
 266: 	struct pcc2_data *d = (struct pcc2_data *) extra;
 267: 
 268: 	/*  0xfff42000..0xfff42fff, but only 0x40 unique registers:  */
 269: 	relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE;
 270: 
 271: 	if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
 272: 		idata = memory_readmax64(cpu, data, len);
 273: 
 274: 	if (writeflag == MEM_READ) {
 275: 		if (relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
 276: 			memcpy(data, d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, len);
 277: 		else {
 278: 			static int warned = 0;	/* #119: loud-once (course-correction) */
 279: 			if (!warned) {
 280: 				fatal("[ pcc2: read 0x%x+%i beyond reg space 0x%x; "
 281: 				    "zero-filled ]\n", (int)relative_addr, (int)len,
 282: 				    (int)PCC2_SIZE);
 283: 				warned = 1;
 284: 			}
 285: 			memset(data, 0, len);	/* OB-11b: don't leak uninit host memory */
 286: 		}
 287: 	}
 288: 
 289: 	switch (relative_addr) {
 290: 
 291: 	case PCCTWO_CHIPID:
 292: 	case PCCTWO_CHIPREV:
 293: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 294: 			fatal("[ IGNORING write to read-only PCCTWO_CHIPID/"
 295: 			    "CHIPREV register ]\n");
 296: 		}
 297: 		break;
 298: 
 299: 	case PCCTWO_GENCTL:
 300: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 301: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_GENCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_GENCTL", len);
 302: 			break;
 303: 		}
 304: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 305: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata;
 306: 			reassert_interrupts(d);
 307: 		}
 308: 		break;
 309: 
 310: 	case PCCTWO_VECBASE:
 311: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 312: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN, "PCCTWO_VECBASE", len);
 313: 			break;
 314: 		}
 315: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 316: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] = idata & 0xf0;
 317: 			if (idata & ~0xf0)
 318: 				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_VECBASE"
 319: 				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);
 320: 		}
 321: 		break;
 322: 
 323: 	case PCCTWO_T1CMP:
 324: 	case PCCTWO_T2CMP:
 325: 	case PCCTWO_T1COUNT:
 326: 	case PCCTWO_T2COUNT:
 327: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE &&
 328: 		    relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)	/* OB-11: end-span */
 329: 			memcpy(d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, data, len);
 330: 		break;
 331: 
 332: 	case PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ:
 333: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 334: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_PSCALEADJ_LEN, "PCCTWO_PSCALEADJ", len);
 335: 			break;
 336: 		}
 337: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 338: 			fatal("[ pcc2: write to PSCALEADJ ignored (value "
 339: 			    "0x%02x) ]\n", (int)idata);
 340: 		}
 341: 		break;
 342: 
 343: 	case PCCTWO_T2CTL:
 344: 	case PCCTWO_T1CTL:
 345: 		if (len != 1 && writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 346: 			pcc2_width_decline(d, PCC2_W_TXCTL_LEN, "PCCTWO_TxCTL", len);
 347: 			break;
 348: 		}
 349: 		if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE) {
 350: 			/*  PCC2_TCTL_CEN and PCC2_TCTL_COC:  */
 351: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &= 0xfc;
 352: 			d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] |= (idata & 3);
 353: 
 354: 			if (idata & PCC2_TCTL_COVF)
 355: 				d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr] &=
 356: 				    ~(PCC2_TCTL_OVF | PCC2_TCTL_COVF);
 357: 
 358: 			if (idata & ~7)
 359: 				fatal("[ pcc2: HUH? write to PCCTWO_TxCTL"
 360: 				    " with value 0x%02x. ]\n", (int) idata);

 succeeded in 6851ms:
   1: # #501 pcc2defaultkill -- PASS 2: the nine-seat pass on the REAL DIFF.  The `default:` arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) ended the emulator process on any access to an offset with no `case`, reads included; it now keeps served reads and declines writes, one latched announce per offset per instance.
   2: 
   3: ## READ THIS FIRST -- PLEASE DO NOT SIMPLY RATIFY
   4: 
   5: The pass-1 panel (nine seats, 2026-09-05 02:17-03:27) converged on this design and found two premises of the pass-1
   6: brief wrong (the guest driver never attaches on this model; the conf excerpt was empty because the grep was wrong)
   7: and one witness disposition-blind (13/13 under BOTH candidate fixes).  This pass reviews what was BUILT: the product
   8: diff, the 23-row detector, the gate section, the kill table, and the record.  **Check the premises, not just the
   9: derivations; be harder on the accepted dispositions than the rejected ones; a seat whose position was adopted
  10: (Opus's fix A) scrutinises it MORE.**  Anything you can check against the tree, check.  **VERDICT LINE FIRST.**
  11: 
  12: **THE TREE UNDER REVIEW IS `GXEMUL-SEC/src/` and `GXEMUL-SEC/regress/`, UNCOMMITTED on top of `7076134`.**  Three
  13: pristine-2021 copies of `src/` under the project root are NOT this fork; if your line numbers disagree with mine by
  14: hundreds you are reading one -- say so.  The guest source is `_guest-src-openbsd22/`; the MVME187 board manual is
  15: `_scratchpad/vmesbcp2.txt` (fetched 2026-09-05 at the codex seat's citation).
  16: 
  17: ## PREMISES ALREADY SETTLED, RE-INLINED (each is a QUESTION if you can check it)
  18: 
  19: 1. **43 of the 0x40 byte offsets reach `default:`** -- 18 named unimplemented registers, 12 counter-word interior
  20:    bytes, 13 unnamed (derived from mvme_pcctworeg.h against the 21 case labels; two seats re-derived it).
  21: 2. **Reads are served BEFORE the switch** by the memcpy (with the #119 end-span guard: a read crossing 0x40 is
  22:    zero-filled, not served -- irrelevant to byte offsets, and premise 2 of the pass-1 brief was over-general there).
  23: 3. **The witness** (`witness_20260905/`): on the committed build a GUEST `st.b` or `ld.bu` to IEICR ends the host
  24:    (exit 1) with the arm's own `pcc2: unimplemented` line first; IEBERR, the in-word byte 0x05 and the alias 0x6a
  25:    likewise; the debugger's `put b` does NOT reach the arm (a control -- `put b` never reaches a DM_DEFAULT device).
  26:    7/13 pre-fix, 13/13 post-fix (both inlined).  The IEBERR/IEERR row measures IEBERR only (the exiting build dies at
  27:    the first); IEERR's kill follows from source (codex).  **The witness is DISPOSITION-BLIND** (Opus built both
  28:    candidates: 13/13 under decline and under backed-accept) -- the DETECTOR's `ieicr rdawr`/`ieerr rdawr` rows (store
  29:    0x5a then load in ONE session, want 0x0) are what pin the disposition; `sccerr rdawr` (a CASED write-to-clear
  30:    sibling, 0x0 under either) is the control that makes the delta attributable.
  31: 4. **The guest driver never attaches on this model**: `machine_mvme88k.c:106` installs `unreadable` at 0xfff46000
  32:    (the ie0 address), `if_ie.c:407-409` fails its match on `badpaddr()` there, and `ie_setupram()` returns early
  33:    at `:561-564` before the PCC2 store at `:609`.  `ie0` IS configured in `conf/M187` and `conf/NFS` (the pass-1
  34:    brief's conf grep looked in the wrong files).  So the HIGH ranking rests on the witness and severity.
  35: 5. **The alias is DOCUMENTED**: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists `$FFF42040-$FFF42FFF  PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated`
  36:    (`_scratchpad/vmesbcp2.txt` line 4629), so `relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE` models the part.  The fork's
  37:    `PCC2_SIZE` (0x40) and the guest header's (0x1000) name different things.
  38: 6. **The design, converged**: READ side keeps the byte the memcpy served and says so (`served 0x%02x` -- it is the
  39:    array's default, NOT a computed value: not member 4 of the option set); WRITE side member 3 -- DECLINE + latched
  40:    `fatal()` `(once)`, per OFFSET (one `uint64_t` bit per byte offset; `relative_addr` is folded to 0..0x3f above
  41:    the switch so the shift is defined).  NOT member 6 (backed accept): the cased siblings contradict a blanket store
  42:    (SCCERR is write-to-clear -- the manual's SCLR bits read as zero; the ICRs mask + reassert; PSCALECNT/SPEED/the
  43:    IACKs are read-only), the addition licence is unmet, and a store from the one arm 43 offsets reach adds a
  44:    bounded-write surface.  The codex seat's alternative -- ONE latch for the whole site, writes only, reads keep
  45:    the old `debug()` -- is RECORDED, not adopted (per-offset announces are what a driver scanning registers needs;
  46:    both are bounded).
  47: 7. **The IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest**; modelling them (on the SCCRXIACK shape) would open
  48:    two new entries into the C040 path, so `pcc2c040kill` goes first (filed as a cross-round obligation).  The
  49:    in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters -- declined here as a width matter, disclosed, not
  50:    claimed as unimplemented registers.
  51: 8. **`return 0` denotes a FAILED TRANSACTION**, not inherently unmapped hardware (an IACK cycle with no interrupt
  52:    pending times out on the real part) -- a correction to the option set's member-5 wording (codex).
  53: 9. **The roster**: all nine seats; agy DELIBERATELY NOT FIRED (measurement reserve).
  54:

+		/*  #501 (pcc2defaultkill): this arm ended the host with exit(1) on ANY
+		    access to an offset with no case -- 43 of the 0x40 byte offsets:
+		    eighteen documented registers the model does not implement (IEICR,
+		    IEERR, IEBERR, GPIO, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, the *IACKs),
+		    the bytes inside the four counter/compare words, and the unnamed
+		    ones -- READS included, though the memcpy above the switch had
+		    already served them.  A guest ld.bu or st.b at any of them was a host
+		    kill (rung-3 witness).  Now: a READ keeps the served byte (the array's
+		    default, said so -- not a computed value); a WRITE is DECLINED.  Not a
+		    backed store: the cased siblings contradict one (SCCERR clears on
+		    write, the ICRs mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are
+		    read-only), no guest reaches these offsets on this model (if_ie never
+		    attaches: unreadable sits at its 82596 address), and a store from the

+		    latched fatal() per offset per instance, the house `(once)' shape --

+		    PCCchip2 registers.  */

+			if (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)
+				fatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "

 736: ## THE CHANGELOG BLOCK (as it stands)
 737: 
 738: ```
 739: # Round #501 pcc2defaultkill -- the `default:` arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) ended the host on any access to an uncased offset, reads included; now reads keep the served byte and writes are declined, one latched announce per offset
 740: 
 741: **2026-09-05.  HIGH, ranked first by the 2026-09-04 fix-review adjudication.  Product change: `src/devices/dev_pcc2.c`
 742: -- the `exit(1)` replaced by a per-offset latched `fatal()` (`uint64_t warned_unimpl`, one bit per byte offset) that
 743: says DECLINED for a write and SERVED 0x%02x for a read, then `break` to the handler's single `return 1`.  Detector:
 744: `regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, 23 rows, run by gate 14.  Witness: rung 3, measured before the edit.**
 745: 
 746: ## The defect and its witness
 747: 
 748: Forty-three of the 0x40 byte offsets have no `case` (derived from `mvme_pcctworeg.h` against the 21 labels; two seats
 749: re-derived it): eighteen NAMED registers the model does not implement (PSCALECNT, GPIO_ICR/PCR, SCCMOIACK, SCCTXIACK,
 750: IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, PRTDATA), the twelve bytes INSIDE the four 32-bit
 751: counter/compare words, and thirteen unnamed offsets.  Any access to one of them -- a READ included, although the
 752: memcpy above the switch had already served it -- reached `default:` and `exit(1)`.
 753: 
 754: **The witness (`regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/`), measured on the committed build BEFORE the
 755: edit: 7/13.**  A guest `st.b` to IEICR (0x2a) ends the host with exit status 1, the arm's own `pcc2: unimplemented`
 756: line printed first (the arm was reached); a guest `ld.bu` there likewise -- the READ side kills too; IEBERR and the
 757: in-word byte 0x05 and the alias 0x6a kill the same way.  Liveness (the probe's scratch round-trip) and
 758: device-signature (CHIPID reads PCC2_ID 0x20) controls pass.  **The debugger's `put b` does NOT reach the arm** -- it
 759: survives and prints nothing -- which the filing had called the cheapest witness (measured false; corrected).  The
 760: witness script is the width probe's own `session()` helper: a first draft hand-rolled the pty wait, accepted a
 761: STALE prompt, and read every kill as "survived" -- the carrier's rule about hand-rolled pty plumbing, re-learned.
 762: The combined IEBERR/IEERR row measures IEBERR only (the exiting build dies at the first); IEERR's kill follows from
 763: source (codex).  **13/13 on the fixed build.**
 764: 
 765: ## The design (pass 1: nine seats, `_scratchpad/panel_20260905_021751`, converged)
 766: 
 767: READ: keep the byte the memcpy served and SAY it is served -- it is the register array's default, not a computed
 768: value, so this is not the option set's member 4 and a bounded announce about it is not a wrong record.  WRITE:
 769: member 3, DECLINE + latched announce -- NOT member 6 (backed accept), on three warrants the seats measured or read:
 770: the file's own cased siblings contradict a blanket store (SCCERR is write-to-clear -- the board manual's SCLR bits
 771: read as zero; the ICRs mask and reassert; PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are read-only); the addition licence is unmet
 772: (no guest reaches these offsets on this model); a store from the one arm 43 offsets reach adds a bounded-write
 773: surface into `pcctwo_reg[]` at 0x3d.  The Opus seat BUILT both candidates in a scratch clone and measured the
 774: 13-row witness at 13/13 under BOTH -- disposition-blind -- so the detector carries read-after-write rows in ONE
 775: session (`ieicr rdawr`, `ieerr rdawr`: store 0x5a then load, want 0x0 -- a backed accept reads 0x5a) with the CASED
 776: write-to-clear sibling `sccerr rdawr` as the control that reads 0x0 under either.
 777: 
 778: **Two premises of the pass-1 brief were wrong and are corrected here.**  The in-tree guest driver (`if_ie.c`) writes
 779: three of these offsets, but NEVER RUNS on this model: `machine_mvme88k.c:106` installs `unreadable` at the 82596
 780: address, `if_ie.c:407-409` fails its match on `badpaddr()` there, and `ie_setupram()` returns early at `:561-564`
 781: before the PCC2 store at `:609` (Fable, Opus, grok, codex independently; verified).  `ie0` IS configured in
 782: `conf/M187` and `conf/NFS` -- the brief's conf grep looked in the wrong files.  So the HIGH ranking rests on the
 783: witness and severity, not on the driver.  And the alias the model implements (`relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE`) is the
 784: REAL part's: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists `$FFF42040-$FFF42FFF  PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated` -- the codex seat
 785: cited it, the manual was fetched into `_scratchpad/` and the line read (`vmesbcp2.txt:4629`).  Two seats had rightly
 786: called the fold unestablished before the manual was on disk.
 787: 
 788: Recorded alternatives: the codex seat's ONE latch for the whole site, writes only (not adopted: a driver scanning
 789: registers should see each distinct one announced; both forms are bounded).  Corrections to the option set from this
 790: round: `return 0` denotes a FAILED TRANSACTION, not inherently unmapped hardware (an IACK cycle with no interrupt
 791: pending times out on the real part).  Disclosed: the in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters, declined
 792: here as a width/alignment matter (`pcc2widecompose`), not claimed as unimplemented registers; the IACK offsets
 793: 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest -- modelling them on the SCCRXIACK shape would open two new entries into the
 794: C040 path, so `pcc2c040kill` goes first (filed as a cross-round obligation); per-instance latch scope is not
 795: provable by two fresh sessions (each is a new process) -- a two-instance fixture is filed.
 796: 
 797: ## The detector (`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, 23 rows; gate 14 pins them)
 798: 
 799: Controls: `live`, `devsig chipid`, `sccerr rdawr` (cased sibling), `genctl rt`, `t1cmp word rt` (the cased arms are
 800: untouched), `put b ctl` + `put b ctl msg` (the debugger path never reaches the arm).  Survival: `named wr`, `named rd`
 801: (all eighteen named offsets in ONE session per direction -- valid because the fix has no per-offset branch;
 802: `named wr msg` localises), `inword wr`, `unnamed wr`, `alias 0x6a`.  Disposition: `ieicr rdawr`, `ieerr rdawr` (0x0,
 803: not the stored byte), `named rd srvd` (the read announce says SERVED, never ignored).  Latch: `named wr msg` 18,
 804: `named rd msg` 18, `latch sameoff` 1, `latch twooff` 2, `latch rw one` 1.  Text: `alias msg` (the announce names the
 805: FOLDED offset), `dir wr text`, `dir rd text`.  The pty helper is the width probe's, extracted at run time -- the
 806: frozen instrument is therefore a PAIR (both files copied beside the table).

 870: ## OPERATIONAL RULE FOR EVERY SEAT (appended by panel.sh, not by the author)
 871: 
 872: **Do not run `run.sh`, any `gate_*.sh`, or the battery.** Every measurement you need is
 873: inlined above. A gate takes minutes -- longer than your response budget -- and a second
 874: concurrent invocation corrupts the operator run in progress, because the gates share one
 875: log directory. A seat that runs a gate produces no review AND may invalidate someone
 876: else s. Building and running a single detector or mutant in your own temp directory is
 877: fine and encouraged; driving the harness is not.
 878: 
 879: If you want a measurement that is not inlined, NAME IT and say why. Naming it is worth
 880: far more to this panel than a timeout.

 554: ## THE DETECTOR (`regress/pcc2_default_probe.py`, VERBATIM, contract included)
 555: 
 556: ```python
 557: #!/usr/bin/env python3
 558: """pcc2defaultkill DETECTOR (#501) -- the `default:' arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) in dev_pcc2.c.
 559: 
 560: Before #501 that arm ended the emulator process (`exit(1)') on ANY access to an offset with no `case'
 561: -- READS included, although the memcpy above the switch had already served them.  Forty-three of the
 562: 0x40 byte offsets reach it (derived from mvme_pcctworeg.h against the case list): eighteen NAMED
 563: registers the model does not implement (PSCALECNT, GPIO_ICR/PCR, SCCMOIACK, SCCTXIACK, IEERR, IEICR,
 564: IEBERR, SCSIERR, the printer block, SPEED, PRTDATA), the bytes INSIDE the four 32-bit counter/compare
 565: words, and the unnamed offsets.  A single guest `st.b' or `ld.bu' at any of them was a host kill
 566: (rung-3 witness, 7/13 on the committed build: regress/artifacts/pcc2defaultkill/witness_20260905/).
 567: 
 568: THE FIX (pass-1 panel of 2026-09-05, nine seats, converged): READ side -- keep the byte the memcpy
 569: already served (it is the register array's default, NOT a computed value: the announce says so);
 570: WRITE side -- DECLINE + a per-offset latched `fatal()' ("(once)", the house idiom), NOT a backed
 571: accept: the file's own cased siblings contradict a blanket store (SCCERR is write-to-clear, the ICRs
 572: mask and reassert, PSCALECNT/SPEED/the IACKs are read-only), the addition licence is unmet (no guest
 573: reaches these offsets on this model -- if_ie never attaches), and a store into pcctwo_reg[] from the
 574: one arm reached by 43 offsets adds a bounded-write surface the decline does not have.
 575: 
 576: CONTRACT -- what this detector is built to catch, by row:
 577:   (b) the verbatim `exit(1)' reinstated             -> every survival row (named wr/rd, inword, unnamed, alias)
 578:   (d) the announce unlatched                        -> `latch sameoff' (2 lines, not 1)
 579:   (d) one whole-arm latch bit instead of per-offset -> `latch twooff' (1 line, not 2), `named wr msg' (1, not 18)
 580:   (d) a writeflag guard that re-kills READS         -> `named rd' (host exit)
 581:   (d) the direction test inverted in the text       -> `dir wr text' / `dir rd text'
 582:   member 6 (BACKED ACCEPT) substituted for member 3 -> `ieicr rdawr' / `ieerr rdawr' read back the stored
 583:       byte (0x5a) instead of 0x0 -- the DISPOSITION-pinning rows; `sccerr rdawr' (a CASED, write-to-clear
 584:       sibling) is the control that reads 0x0 under either and makes the delta attributable
 585:   the `relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE' fold changed      -> `alias msg' (the announce names offset 0x2a, not 0x6a)
 586:   the fix perturbing a cased arm                    -> `genctl rt', `t1cmp word rt'
 587: DISCLOSED, not caught: the in-word bytes are the interior of IMPLEMENTED counters, declined here as a
 588: width/alignment matter (pcc2widecompose's business), not as unimplemented registers; the fold of
 589: 0x40..0xfff onto 0x00..0x3f IS the real part's: Motorola VMESBCP2 Table 6-1 lists $FFF42040-$FFF42FFF as
 590: 'PCCchip2 Registers  Repeated' (_scratchpad/vmesbcp2.txt:4629, fetched at the codex seat's citation -- two
 591: seats had rightly called it unestablished before the manual was on disk), so `alias 0x6a' pins a documented
 592: behaviour; the fork's PCC2_SIZE (0x40) and the guest header's (0x1000) name different things;
 593: the IACK offsets 0x23/0x25 are declined like the rest -- modelling them (on the SCCRXIACK shape) would
 594: open two new entries into the C040 path, so pcc2c040kill goes first; `named wr'/`named rd' touch all
 595: eighteen offsets in ONE session each (valid because the fix has no per-offset branch; `named wr msg'
 596: localises a failure) -- if a later round branches per offset this compression is invalid.
 597: The debugger's `put b' never reaches a DM_DEFAULT arm (measured): `put b ctl' is a control, not a witness.
 598: 
 599: Usage: python3 pcc2_default_probe.py <gxemul> <label>      Sentinel: PCC2D_PROBE_DONE
 600: The pty session helper is the width probe's own (extracted at run time), never hand-rolled: a first
 601: draft of the witness hand-rolled it and read every host kill as "survived".
 602: """
 603: import io, os, pty, re, select, sys, time
 604: BIN = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "build/gxemul"
 605: LABEL = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else "local"
 606: DUMMY = "/tmp/pcc2_dummy.bin"
 607: if not os.path.exists(DUMMY):
 608:     open(DUMMY, "wb").write(b"\0" * 4096)
 609: HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
 610: _w = io.open(os.path.join(HERE, "pcc2_width_probe.py"), encoding="utf-8").read()
 611: exec(_w[_w.index("def session("):_w.index("\ndef printed")])     # session(cmds) -> (transcript, alive, how)
 612: 
 613: CODE, SCRATCH, PCC2, SENTINEL = 0x00010000, 0x00020000, 0xfff42000, 0xdeadbeef
 614: CHIPID, GENCTL, T1CMP, SCCERR = 0x00, 0x02, 0x04, 0x1c
 615: IEERR, IEICR, IEBERR, SCCTXIACK, PRTICR = 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x25, 0x30
 616: NAMED18 = [0x14, 0x18, 0x19, 0x23, 0x25, 0x28, 0x2a, 0x2b, 0x2c, 0x30, 0x31, 0x32, 0x33, 0x34, 0x36, 0x37, 0x38, 0x3a]
 617: INWORD = [0x05, 0x09, 0x0d, 0x11]
 618: UNNAMED = [0x20, 0x24, 0x29, 0x35, 0x3d]
 619: MSG = "pcc2: unimplemented"
 620: def enc(op26, rd, rs1, imm): return (op26 << 26) | (rd << 21) | (rs1 << 16) | (imm & 0xffff)
 621: def LD_BU(rd, off): return enc(0x03, rd, 2, off)
 622: def LD_W(rd, off): return enc(0x05, rd, 2, off)
 623: def ST_B(rs, off): return enc(0x0b, rs, 2, off)
 624: def ST_W(rs, off): return enc(0x09, rs, 2, off)
 625: I_ST_SCR = enc(0x09, 5, 3, 0); I_LD_SCR = enc(0x05, 4, 3, 0); I_BR_SELF = 0xc0000000
 626: def program(insns, at=CODE):
 627:     words = list(insns) + [I_BR_SELF, 0]
 628:     return ["put w 0x%x, 0x%08x" % (at + 4 * k, w) for k, w in enumerate(words)] + ["pc=0x%x" % at, "step %d" % len(insns)]
 629: def setup(r5="0x5a5a5a5a"):
 630:     return ["r2=0x%x" % PCC2, "r3=0x%x" % SCRATCH, "r5=%s" % r5, "r4=0x%x" % SENTINEL]
 631: def printed(t, reg="r4"):
 632:     m = re.search(r"print\s+%s\s*\r?\n\s*(0x[0-9a-f]+)" % reg, t)
 633:     return "0x%x" % int(m.group(1), 16) if m else "absent"
 634: def fate(alive, how): return "survived" if alive else "host " + how
 635: def val(t, alive, how): return printed(t) if alive else fate(alive, how)
 636: rows, bad = [], []
 637: def row(name, got, want, why):
 638:     ok = str(got) == str(want)
 639:     rows.append(name); (None if ok else bad.append(name))
 640:     #  STATUS LAST: gate 14 counts a row with `^<name>  .*ok$' / ` ok$' / ` FAIL$' -- a first draft printed the
 641:     #  status before the note and the gate counted ZERO of 23 rows while the probe said DONE (caught by the gate, L1).
 642:     print("%-16s %-14s %-14s %-60s %s" % (name, got, want, why[:60], "ok" if ok else "FAIL"), flush=True)
 643: print("pcc2defaultkill DETECTOR -- binary=%s label=%s" % (BIN, LABEL))
 644: print("%-16s %-14s %-14s %-60s %s" % ("row", "got", "want", "note", ""))
 645: 
 646: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([I_ST_SCR, I_LD_SCR]) + ["print r4"])
 647: row("live", val(t, alive, how), "0x5a5a5a5a", "LIVENESS: scratch RAM st/ld round-trip")
 648: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, CHIPID)]) + ["print r4"])
 649: row("devsig chipid", val(t, alive, how), "0x20", "DEVICE-SIGNATURE: CHIPID reads PCC2_ID through a guest ld.bu (RAM would read 0)")
 650: # -- the eighteen named unimplemented registers, one session each direction
 651: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, o) for o in NAMED18]))
 652: row("named wr", fate(alive, how), "survived", "guest st.b at all 18 named unimplemented registers in ONE session: (b) reinstated exit(1) kills on the first")
 653: row("named wr msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 18, "one announce PER OFFSET: a whole-arm latch prints 1, an unlatched arm prints 18 too -- see `latch sameoff'")
 654: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, o) for o in NAMED18]))
 655: row("named rd", fate(alive, how), "survived", "guest ld.bu at all 18 in one session: the READ side of the defect; a writeflag guard that re-kills reads dies here")
 656: row("named rd msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 18, "the read announce fires once per offset too")
 657: row("named rd srvd", t.count("served 0x") if alive else "n/a", 18, "the read announce says what happened -- SERVED (the array's byte), never 'ignored'")
 658: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, o) for o in INWORD]))
 659: row("inword wr", fate(alive, how), "survived", "a byte inside each 32-bit counter/compare word (0x05, 0x09, 0x0d, 0x11): no case -> default:")
 660: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, o) for o in UNNAMED]))
 661: row("unnamed wr", fate(alive, how), "survived", "unnamed offsets 0x20, 0x24, 0x29, 0x35, 0x3d -- 0x3d is the one a backed store at len 4 would overrun")
 662: # -- the disposition-pinning rows: read-after-write in ONE session
 663: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), LD_BU(4, IEICR)]) + ["print r4"])
 664: row("ieicr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "st.b 0x5a then ld.bu at IEICR in ONE session reads 0x0: the write was DECLINED (a backed accept reads 0x5a)")
 665: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEERR), LD_BU(4, IEERR)]) + ["print r4"])
 666: row("ieerr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "the same at IEERR (0x28), the write-to-clear class whose cased sibling SCCERR clears on write")
 667: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, SCCERR), LD_BU(4, SCCERR)]) + ["print r4"])
 668: row("sccerr rdawr", val(t, alive, how), "0x0", "CONTROL: the CASED write-to-clear sibling reads 0x0 under either disposition -- makes the two rows above attributable")
 669: # -- the latch: per offset, per instance
 670: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEICR)]))
 671: row("latch sameoff", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "two writes to one offset announce ONCE: an unlatched arm prints 2")
 672: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), ST_B(5, IEBERR)]))
 673: row("latch twooff", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 2, "two offsets announce TWICE: a single whole-arm latch bit prints 1")
 674: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, IEICR), LD_BU(4, IEICR)]))
 675: row("latch rw one", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 1, "a write then a read at ONE offset announce once: the latch is per offset, not per direction")
 676: # -- the fold and the text
 677: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, 0x6a)]))
 678: row("alias 0x6a", fate(alive, how), "survived", "0x6a folds onto 0x2a (`relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE'): the MODEL's fold, not proven of the real part")
 679: row("alias msg", t.count("offset 0x2a") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and the announce names the FOLDED offset 0x2a: a changed fold names another")
 680: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x5a") + program([ST_B(5, PRTICR)]))
 681: row("dir wr text", t.count("unimplemented write to") if alive else "n/a", 1, "the announce says WRITE for a store (an inverted writeflag test in the text says read)")
 682: t, alive, how = session(setup() + program([LD_BU(4, PRTICR)]))
 683: row("dir rd text", t.count("unimplemented read from") if alive else "n/a", 1, "...and READ for a load")
 684: # -- the cased arms are untouched
 685: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x82") + program([ST_B(5, GENCTL), LD_BU(4, GENCTL)]) + ["print r4"])
 686: row("genctl rt", val(t, alive, how), "0x82", "an IMPLEMENTED byte register still round-trips (no C040 bit in 0x82)")
 687: t, alive, how = session(setup(r5="0x12345678") + program([ST_W(5, T1CMP), LD_W(4, T1CMP)]) + ["print r4"])
 688: row("t1cmp word rt", val(t, alive, how), "0x12345678", "the cased 32-bit counter arm still round-trips a word: the fix swallowed no cased arm")
 689: t, alive, how = session(["put b 0xfff4202a, 0x00"])
 690: row("put b ctl", fate(alive, how), "survived", "CONTROL: the debugger's put b never reaches a DM_DEFAULT arm (measured before the fix too)")
 691: row("put b ctl msg", t.count(MSG) if alive else "n/a", 0, "...so it prints nothing -- not a witness, a control")
 692: print("PCC2D_RESULT=%d/%d" % (len(rows) - len(bad), len(rows)))
 693: if bad:
 694:     print("PCC2D_RED=%s" % ",".join(bad))
 695: print("PCC2D_PROBE_DONE")
 696: 
 697: ```

#!/bin/bash
#  #501 pcc2defaultkill KILL TABLE -- the new default: arm of DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2), six mutants, the 23-row
#  detector regress/pcc2_default_probe.py (frozen with the width probe it borrows session() from).
#    BASE       23/23 owed (the fix)
#    M_EXIT     (b) verbatim exit(1) reinstated
#    M_UNLATCH  (d) announce unlatched
#    M_ONELATCH (d) one whole-arm latch bit
#    M_BACKED   member 6 (a store into pcctwo_reg[]) substituted
#    M_RDKILL   (d) reads re-killed
#    M_DIRSWAP  (d) the announce's direction test inverted
set -u
G=/mnt/c/DocumentNoSnc/CC/GXEMUL; SEC=$G/GXEMUL-SEC; T=/tmp/gxdk501; F=src/devices/dev_pcc2.c
OUT=/tmp/killdk501; PROBE=pcc2_default_probe.py
say() { echo "[$(date +%H:%M:%S)] $*"; }
die() { echo "*** ABORT: $*"; exit 9; }
rm -rf "$T" "$OUT"; mkdir -p "$OUT"
cp -a "$G/build" "$T" || die "clone failed"
cmp -s "$T/$F" "$SEC/$F" || { cp "$SEC/$F" "$T/$F"; say "  clone's $F refreshed from SEC (comment-only change since build/ was built)"; }
cp "$SEC/regress/$PROBE" "$OUT/frozen_probe.py" || die "cannot freeze probe"
cp "$SEC/regress/pcc2_width_probe.py" "$OUT/pcc2_width_probe.py" || die "cannot freeze the session helper source"
say "  instrument frozen: $(wc -c < "$OUT/frozen_probe.py") bytes"
build() { cd "$T" || die cd; rm -f src/devices/dev_pcc2.o; make -j4 > "$OUT/build_$1.log" 2>&1; [ "$T/gxemul" -nt "$T/$F" ] || die "$1: binary not newer than source"; }
score() {
    cd "$SEC/regress" || die cd
    python3 -u "$OUT/frozen_probe.py" "$T/gxemul" "$1" > "$OUT/$1.out" 2>&1
    grep -q PCC2D_PROBE_DONE "$OUT/$1.out" || die "$1: NO COMPLETION SENTINEL"
    say "  $1 [$2]: $(grep -m1 PCC2D_RESULT "$OUT/$1.out")"
    grep -m1 PCC2D_RED "$OUT/$1.out" | sed 's/PCC2D_RED=/      RED: /' || true
}
mut() {
    echo "$F  mutant $1" > "$T/.MUTANT"; cp "$SEC/$F" "$T/$F"
    python3 - "$T/$F" "$3" <<'PYEOF'
import io, sys
p, body = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2]; s = io.open(p, encoding='utf-8').read()
ns = {'s': s}; exec(body, ns); io.open(p, 'w', encoding='utf-8', newline='\n').write(ns['s'])
PYEOF
    [ $? -eq 0 ] || die "$1: mutator failed"
    cmp -s "$T/$F" "$SEC/$F" && die "$1: file UNCHANGED after mutation"
    MD=$(diff "$SEC/$F" "$T/$F" | grep -c "^[<>]"); say "    [$1] mutant diff vs SEC: $MD lines; first added: $(diff "$SEC/$F" "$T/$F" | grep "^>" | head -1 | cut -c1-100)"; [ "$MD" -gt 0 ] || die "$1: MUTATION NOT APPLIED (source identical to SEC)"
    build "$1"
    say "    [$1] binary $(date -r "$T/gxemul" +%H:%M:%S)  source $(date -r "$T/$F" +%H:%M:%S)"
    score "$1" "$2"
}
say "BASE -- 23/23 owed"; build BASE; score BASE CONTROL
BASE_RES=$(grep -m1 PCC2D_RESULT "$OUT/BASE.out" | cut -d= -f2)
[ "$BASE_RES" = "23/23" ] || die "BASE IS NOT FULL ($BASE_RES): $(grep -m1 PCC2D_RED "$OUT/BASE.out") -- a table on a broken instrument measures nothing (tablebasenotfull)"
say "RESTORE from SEC + REBUILD + verify, THEN clear the sentinel"
DKN='\t\tif (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {\n\t\t\td->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;\n\t\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x: 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr,\n\t\t\t\t    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);\n\t\t}\n\t\tbreak;\n'
DKO='\t\tdebug("[ pcc2: unimplemented %s offset 0x%x",\n\t\t    writeflag == MEM_WRITE? "write to" : "read from",\n\t\t    (int) relative_addr);\n\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)\n\t\t\tdebug(": 0x%x", (int)idata);\n\t\tdebug(" ]\\n");\n\t\texit(1);\n'
DKU='\t\tif (1) {\n\t\t\td->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;\n\t\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x: 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr,\n\t\t\t\t    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);\n\t\t}\n\t\tbreak;\n'
DK1='\t\tif (!(d->warned_unimpl & 1ULL)) {\n\t\t\td->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL;\n\t\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x: 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr,\n\t\t\t\t    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);\n\t\t}\n\t\tbreak;\n'
DKB='\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE && relative_addr + len <= PCC2_SIZE)\n\t\t\tmemcpy(d->pcctwo_reg + relative_addr, data, len);\n\t\tif (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {\n\t\t\td->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;\n\t\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x: 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr,\n\t\t\t\t    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);\n\t\t}\n\t\tbreak;\n'
DKR='\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_READ)\n\t\t\texit(1);\n\t\tif (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {\n\t\t\td->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;\n\t\t\tif (writeflag == MEM_WRITE)\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x: 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr,\n\t\t\t\t    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);\n\t\t}\n\t\tbreak;\n'
DKS='\t\tif (!(d->warned_unimpl & (1ULL << relative_addr))) {\n\t\t\td->warned_unimpl |= 1ULL << relative_addr;\n\t\t\tif (writeflag != MEM_WRITE)\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented write to offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x: 0x%x -- declined.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr, (int) idata);\n\t\t\telse\n\t\t\t\tfatal("[ pcc2: unimplemented read from offset "\n\t\t\t\t    "0x%x -- served 0x%02x.  (once) ]\\n",\n\t\t\t\t    (int) relative_addr,\n\t\t\t\t    d->pcctwo_reg[relative_addr]);\n\t\t}\n\t\tbreak;\n'
mut M_EXIT 'every survival row red (host exit1): named wr/rd, inword wr, unnamed wr, alias 0x6a, both rdawr rows; genctl/t1cmp/put b/live/devsig stay -- (b) verbatim reinstatement' "a='$DKN'; assert s.count(a)==1; s=s.replace(a,'$DKO')"
mut M_UNLATCH 'latch sameoff 2, latch rw one 2 -- the announce fires on every access' "a='$DKN'; assert s.count(a)==1; s=s.replace(a,'$DKU')"
mut M_ONELATCH 'named wr msg 1, named rd msg 1, named rd srvd 1, latch twooff 1 -- a single whole-arm latch bit' "a='$DKN'; assert s.count(a)==1; s=s.replace(a,'$DK1')"
mut M_BACKED 'ieicr rdawr 0x5a, ieerr rdawr 0x5a; sccerr rdawr stays 0x0 (the control) -- member 6 substituted for member 3' "a='$DKN'; assert s.count(a)==1; s=s.replace(a,'$DKB')"
mut M_RDKILL 'named rd host exit1 (+ rd msg/srvd n/a), dir rd text n/a -- a writeflag guard that re-kills READS' "a='$DKN'; assert s.count(a)==1; s=s.replace(a,'$DKR')"
mut M_DIRSWAP 'dir wr text 0, dir rd text 0, named rd srvd 0 -- the direction test inverted in the announce' "a='$DKN'; assert s.count(a)==1; s=s.replace(a,'$DKS')"
cp "$SEC/$F" "$T/$F"; cmp -s "$T/$F" "$SEC/$F" || die "restore cmp FAILED -- sentinel KEPT"
build restore; rm -f "$T/.MUTANT"; say "  restored + REBUILT + verified, sentinel cleared"
say "KILLDK501_DONE"

  65: // #define	debug fatal
  66: 
  67: /*  pcc2widthkills: TWELVE width-guard blocks in DEVICE_ACCESS(pcc2) -- TEN of them
  68:     `fatal(); exit(1)' kills that ended the host on a guest-chosen access width, and
  69:     two (IPL, MASK) #119 warn-once latches that declined it process-globally -- an ordinary aligned halfword load of a documented
  70:     register.  Same class as #337 (dev_wdc.c) and #497 (dev_sgi_re.c).
  71: 
  72:     THE TREE ALREADY KNEW THE WIDTH IS LEGAL: mvme_pcctworeg.h says the PCCchip2
  73:     registers "can be accessed as bytes (8 bits), two-bytes (16 bits), or four-bytes
  74:     (32 bits)".  So this is not a fault to model; it is an access the model declines
  75:     to IMPLEMENT on one side and already implements on the other:
  76: 
  77:       READS   are served BEFORE the switch by memcpy(data, pcctwo_reg + addr, len),
  78:               raw bytes in address order -- width-correct by construction.  The old
  79:               guard discarded a correct answer.  Now: ACCEPT (fall through).
  80:       WRITES  ten of the twelve arms use `idata', the composed value (SCCERR stores a
  81:               constant and IPL ignores it; both decline too -- a wide write's other
  82:               lanes have no home), and `idata' is "the addressed
  83:               byte" ONLY at len == 1: memory_readmax64 composes big-endian, so
  84:               (uint8_t) idata is the HIGHEST-address lane.  Deleting the guard would
  85:               store the neighbour's byte -- st.h 0xAABB to GENCTL would set GENCTL to
  86:               0xBB, VECBASE's lane.  And eight arms mask, read-modify-write or clear
  87:               on write, and seven of the twelve blocks call reassert_interrupts()
  88:               (five of those eight; a seat measured the earlier "six" wrong).  Now: DECLINE +
  89:               latched announce.  Per-lane composition through each arm (COMPOSE) is
  90:               the faithful upgrade and is FILED as pcc2widecompose; not taken here
  91:               because the hardware's lane semantics are not on disk and no driver
  92:               MEASUREMENT exists either way: the only consumer is mvme187, no mvme187
  93:               boot runs in this harness, and the one OS source in the tree (OpenBSD
  94:               2.2) declares these registers `volatile u_char' and byte-accesses them
  95:               -- a reading, not a measurement.
  96: 
  97:     ONE BIT PER BLOCK, twelve, per instance: a driver bringing up the chip touches
  98:     several of these in one pass, and one latch would announce the first and silence
  99:     the rest (#497's own reasoning).  Auto-incremented rather than `1 << N' literals,
 100:     which this fork records as collision-prone; PCC2_W_NBITS names the count, and the
 101:     typedef after the enum makes the build fail if it ever exceeds the 32-bit field.
 102: 
 103:     REACHABILITY, verified: every non-byte m88k access is alignment-checked BEFORE
 104:     memory_rw, and `relative_addr %= PCC2_SIZE' keeps parity, so only the SIX
 105:     EVEN-offset blocks (GENCTL, TxCTL, TxICR, SCCERR, SCCTX, IPL) are GUEST-reachable
 106:     (this said "five" until pass 2; IPL at 0x3e is even, and a seat counted); the
 107:     eight odd OFFSETS -- the six odd-only blocks VECBASE, PSCALEADJ, SCCICR, SCCRX,
 108:     SCSIICR, MASK, plus the T1CTL/T1ICR labels of the shared TxCTL/TxICR blocks --
 109:     are OPERATOR-reachable -- the debugger's `put h' warns on a
 110:     misaligned address and stores anyway, MEASURED (artifacts/pcc2widthkills/
 111:     operator_reach_20260903.out: `put h 0xfff42003' ended the host).  All twelve are
 112:     converted; the detector reaches the odd ones through that path.
 113: 
 114:     NOT FIXED HERE, cited so "twelve kills removed" is not read as "file clean":
 115:     pcc2c040kill (a LEGAL byte write of 0x04 to GENCTL still ends the host in
 116:     reassert_interrupts) and pcc2defaultkill (an unlisted offset still ends it).  */
 117: enum {
 118: 	PCC2_W_GENCTL_LEN,
 119: 	PCC2_W_VECBASE_LEN,
 120: 	PCC2_W_PSCALEADJ_LEN,
 121: 	PCC2_W_TXCTL_LEN,	/*  one block serves T1CTL and T2CTL  */
 122: 	PCC2_W_TXICR_LEN,	/*  one block serves T1ICR and T2ICR  */
 123: 	PCC2_W_SCCERR_LEN,
 124: 	PCC2_W_SCCICR_LEN,
 125: 	PCC2_W_SCCTX_LEN,
 126: 	PCC2_W_SCCRX_LEN,
 127: 	PCC2_W_SCSIICR_LEN,
 128: 	PCC2_W_IPL_LEN,		/*  was a #119 function-static: process-global  */
 129: 	PCC2_W_MASK_LEN,	/*  was a #119 function-static: process-global  */
 130: 	PCC2_W_NBITS
 131: };
